## Step 5 — editorial conflict decisions
- beats 180-184 keyword-flash x5 within 4 beats: KEPT DELIBERATELY. This is the five-questions
  checklist; the repeated device is the point (consistent form = one list), not a variety failure.

  overlay entrance has a boundary with real duration (Murch priority: overlay substance > cut rhythm).

## Step 5/6 — style gate warnings, adjudicated (not silently ignored)
- WARN longest gap 7.48s (beat 32): NOT a static hold. Beat 32 is an invented-scene
  ($50,000,000 / 278,000 counters) that animates continuously across its full duration.
  The watchdog measures gaps between SOURCE changes and cannot see intra-scene motion. No change.
- WARN first overlay at 10.91s (beat 04 label-pointer @1.4s): deliberate. Beats 01-03 are the
  cold open ("Go and get your electric bill" / "not the envelope, the bill" / "don't look at what
  you owe") — connective address with no concrete hook. Forcing a card there to satisfy a
  5s target would violate the sparse-by-default rule. No change.
